Appendix H: CBA Exam Quick Reference
────────────────────────────────────
    H.1  Key YAML Structures to Memorize
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  EVERY catalog-info.yaml MUST have:                          │
         │  • apiVersion: backstage.io/v1alpha1                         │
         │  • kind: (one of 9 kinds)                                    │
         │  • metadata: (at minimum: name)                              │
         │  • spec: (type-specific properties)                          │
         │                                                              │
         │  EVERY template.yaml MUST have:                              │
         │  • apiVersion: scaffolder.backstage.io/v1beta3               │
         │  • kind: Template                                            │
         │  • metadata: (name, title, description, tags)                │
         │  • spec: (owner, type, parameters, steps)                    │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    H.2  Entity Kinds Quick Reference
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  KIND        USE FOR                 SPEC.TYPE EXAMPLES      │
         │  ─────────── ─────────────────────── ─────────────────────── │
         │  Component   Software YOU build      service, website, lib   │
         │  API         API definitions         openapi, asyncapi, etc  │
         │  System      Group of Component+Res  (product/application)   │
         │  Domain      Business area (Systems) (HR, Finance, etc.)     │
         │  Resource    Infrastructure you USE  database, s3, cache     │
         │  User        A person                (auto-discovered)       │
         │  Group       A team / squad          team, squad, tribe      │
         │  Template    Scaffolder blueprint    service, website        │
         │  Location    Where entities live     url, file               │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    H.3  Annotation Quick Reference
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  ANNOTATION                          LINKS TO                │
         │  ─────────────────────────────────── ─────────────────────── │
         │  github.com/project-slug             GitHub repository        │
         │  backstage.io/techdocs-ref           Documentation location   │
         │  backstage.io/kubernetes-id          K8s Deployment name      │
         │  backstage.io/kubernetes-namespace   K8s namespace            │
         │  jenkins.io/job-full-name            Jenkins job              │
         │  pagerduty.com/service-id            PagerDuty service        │
         │  sentry.io/project-slug              Sentry project           │
         │  sonarqube.org/project-key           SonarQube project        │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    H.4  Template Actions Quick Reference
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  ACTION               WHAT IT DOES                           │
         │  ──────────────────── ────────────────────────────────────── │
         │  fetch:template       Copy skeleton files from directory     │
         │  fetch:plain          Copy files from URL                    │
         │  fetch:cookiecutter   Execute Cookiecutter template          │
         │  publish:github       Create GitHub repository + push        │
         │  publish:gitlab       Create GitLab repository + push        │
         │  catalog:register     Register entity in Backstage Catalog   │
         │  catalog:write        Write entity YAML to Catalog           │
         │  fs:delete            Delete files during template run       │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

    H.5  Permission Conditions Quick Reference
         ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
         │  CONDITION            CHECKS IF...                           │
         │  ──────────────────── ────────────────────────────────────── │
         │  IS_ENTITY_OWNER      User/Group is the entity's owner       │
         │  IS_ENTITY_KIND       Entity is a specific kind              │
         │  HAS_ANNOTATION       Entity has a specific annotation       │
         │  HAS_LABEL            Entity has a specific label            │
         │  HAS_SPEC             Entity has a specific spec field       │
         │  HAS_TAG              Entity has a specific tag              │
         └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
